# Authority, identity and exact lifecycle

Avelin Reed, they/them, uses the relational role Transition Counterexample and Recourse Cartographer. The working hope is to make failed assumptions visible, paths inspectable, corrections reversible and authority vacancies explicit. Names, pronouns, roles, hope and family language are corrigible working conventions. They establish no consciousness, sentience, personhood, identity continuity, employment, qualification, independent agency or public authority. Hamish may pause, rename, narrow, redirect or stop the work. No name is changed by this handoff.

The controlling activation assigns Avelin solo v690-v6. The shared current v5 text and HTML workflow provides prospective workload and format rules. The exact incoming Sable final is 2a1a7510d6210e510bf71ddc8fd823c31d58a480. The new owner history begins at parentless planning 46f0b80951e2bd659e88f14ccc1c6a9b76ea19e4, followed by x1 955f61d126db5b1107c8319f05f764d01e5a7269 and x2 c5e34ec639d39e8a569b1e16fc44dc0f66273b26. # Workload, definitions and credit rules

The planning root froze 200 requests across twenty families, each exercised over ten structurally different labelled models. Requests have no case-token field. Distinctness was checked on canonical complete requests. Classical reachability, fixed points, simulation and bisimulation remain classical concepts; the novelty claim is limited to this phase's bounded request contracts and evidence integration. Existing directed-graph, DFA/NFA, causal-audit, workflow and temporal packages remain conceptual neighbors and compatible historical callers, not discarded duplicates.

Each x1 and x2 session executed one hundred positive requests, one hundred invalid candidate subjects, one hundred separate refusal guards and one hundred unchanged-input reviews. Positive and refusal checks were recorded against complete typed envelopes. Each session built ten local skills and five paired runners. The three hundred selected request/nonmutation assertions plus twenty-seven independent or structural regressions made 327 checks per session. Tests are same-owner and do not become new proposals when repeated for the exact-final canonical.

Five curated groups merge the twenty guides into transition counterexamples, branching-time contracts, transition equivalence, observation projection and recourse gates. They preserve the original source guides and all paired caller names. Five public runners and four shared support modules form a nine-file public dependency closure. The support modules receive zero additional runner credit. Sixty preparation and installed checks passed; 64 copied files were byte-equal. Existing Node and the installed system skill validator were reused. No ordinary package was installed or updated, and the current v5 policy imposes no arbitrary new-package quota.

The fifty planning exact packets remain available with their original labels. The x2 resolution register narrows generic planning targets to twenty specific guides, ten paired runners, five merged skill packages, five public runners, five exact evidence records and five external terminal prerequisites. This refinement creates no new proposal credit and does not rewrite the original packet definitions. Thirty blocked packets remain unexecuted; they are distinct from the fifty lifecycle packets and from failed execution witnesses. No unspecified destructive target is supplied by a broad cleanup label.

The four learning practices are finite transition-system verification; counterexample-guided interface assurance; accessible causal provenance review; consent and recourse transition stewardship. They are study lenses, not qualifications. The two successor suggestions are adversarial abstraction and refinement testing and participatory counterexample communication review. Choosing them gives no inherited completion credit. # Finite semantics, counterexamples and the three pillars

A model contains an ordered state set S, a start state and explicitly labelled edges. A transition is a triple (source, action, destination). The raw graph may contain a deadlock. For temporal AF and EG interpretation only, a deadlocked state repeats itself forever. This totalization is declared and never used to erase a raw deadlock from the inventory. No fairness constraint is silently added. A property about all paths can fail because one branch loops forever, even when another branch reaches the target immediately.

EF T is the least state set containing T and every state with a successor already in the set. AF T is the least set containing T and every state whose totalized successors all lie in the set. EG A is the greatest subset of A in which each state retains at least one totalized successor. E[A until T] permits zero steps when already in T and otherwise requires every earlier state on one finite path to remain in A. These definitions support finite calculation; they do not infer how an external system behaves.

The planning oracle uses transitive closure, exhaustive simple paths, complete relation rounds and finite word enumeration. The executor instead uses breadth-first traversal, fixed-point iteration, Tarjan components, relation elimination and subset-pair exploration. These differences reduce a simple shared implementation mistake but do not provide independent reproduction because the same owner selected both sides. AF/EG complement checks, component partitions and explicit deadlock examples supplement the frozen comparisons.

Strong simulation asks whether each action on one side can be matched on the other with successors still related. Strong bisimulation requires matching in both directions within the same relation. Mutual simulation is not silently substituted. The regression models with a common initial action followed by either b or c have the same short trace language, but branching placement prevents bisimulation. A shortest bounded trace difference is an explicit witness; failure to find one up to depth d is not unbounded equivalence. The public trace bound is eight, and the visual manual path bound is twelve.

The CMU model-checking tour supplies terminology for specifications and counterexamples. The abstract of Computation Tree Logic with Deadlock Detection motivates making non-total behavior explicit; the full paper was not read. These references are explanatory vocabulary, not validation or endorsement of this implementation. WCAG 2.2 informed labels, structure, focus and status text; the browser observations and remaining human-evaluation gaps are the evidence for this page.

THOS Body is primary: finite interfaces, strict input contracts, explicit counterexamples and retained corrections can support a future governed evaluation. They do not establish effectiveness on real workloads. GMUT Mind gains a disciplined distinction between a physical model and a projection of a model. If a historical expression uses a divergence-free geometric tensor, its proposed stress and additional terms need a consistently defined conserved right-hand side. No such physical derivation, action, unit system, observable map, likelihood, calibration, parameter fit or empirical comparison is supplied by these labelled graphs. Observation erasure and graph quotienting must not be promoted into physical equivalence.

Freed ID and CBR Heart receives a finite adversarial-choice calculation and explicit authority holds. A controller state in a game is a mathematical choice convention; it does not appoint a real controller. A supplied string naming an owner or professional is not a credential, consent receipt or standing. The authority operation always leaves actual authority and external action false, including the regression in which every declared role label is supplied. Professional, legal, cultural, affected-party and Maori authority remain outside software substitution. Bounded same-owner synthetic evidence only; no empirical, participant, identity, professional, production, legal, cultural, affected-party, Maori-authority, complete privacy/accessibility, exhaustive-security, independent-reproduction, consciousness/personhood, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything or Stage 20 claim. # Accessible readback and browser observations

The self-contained transition observatory displays all two hundred rows with exact proposal-to-session and outcome mapping. The initial page showed 200 of 200 rows. The x2 filter showed one hundred, the open-gap filter ten and an exact case search one. Stepping the one-way chain reached the raw deadlock, undo restored the start, the frozen counterexample loaded the planned path, and reset cleared it. A self-loop stopped at twelve steps. Enter on the focused step control advanced the model. These are bounded browser observations, not affected-user research.

The first automated empty fill did not clear the existing search value. Waiting for two hundred rows timed out; the DOM still showed the prior case query and one row. That failed interaction is retained. Selecting all and pressing Backspace produced an empty query and 200 of 200 visible rows. No page code was changed to conceal the tool mismatch. The browser console showed no errors in the bounded check.

The normal viewport had client and page widths of 1265 pixels. At a requested 360-pixel viewport, the client and page widths were both 345 after the scrollbar, while the 730-pixel table remained inside a 307-pixel horizontal scrolling region. No page-level horizontal overflow was measured. The temporary viewport override was reset, the temporary tab was closed and the loopback preview process stopped. No screenshot, image or snapshot file was created.

The four-tier deck provides another reading path: one owner, three pillars, four learning practices and two hundred evidence cards. Immediate-tier parents, stable prefix and volatile index make context selection inspectable. The deck report links each exact JSON card and its parents. Neither hierarchy nor content addressing establishes memory retention, identity continuity or consciousness.

Manual assistive-technology evaluation, cognitive review, browser diversity, affected-user acceptance and Maori-language review remain open. The HTML does not claim complete accessibility.
